<h1>Cheat sheet</h1>
<p class="lead">The syntax of every tag, by group.</p>


<section class="section" id="conditions">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Conditions</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/case">&#123;pad:case&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders the one branch whose {when} matches a value, with an {else} for none.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">case</span> value<span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">when</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;a&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">when</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;b&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-str">&#039;c&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/case</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">case</span> value<span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">when</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;a&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">else</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/case</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/false">&#123;pad:false&#125;</a> <span class="about">The constant FALSE: its content is skipped and its @else@ part renders instead.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">false</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">false</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/false</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">false</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/false</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/if">&#123;pad:if&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders its content only when a condition holds, with elseif and else branches.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">if</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/if</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">if</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">else</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/if</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">if</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">elseif</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">else</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/if</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/ifchanged">&#123;pad:ifchanged&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders its content when a value differs from the previous row of the loop around it - a heading per group.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">ifchanged</span> value<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/ifchanged</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">ifchanged</span> value, value<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/ifchanged</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">ifchanged</span> value<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/ifchanged</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/null">&#123;pad:null&#125;</a> <span class="about">The constant NULL: its content is dropped, the @else@ part too, and a null= option fires.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">null</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">null</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/null</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">null</span> <span class="hl-opt">null</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/null</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/switch">&#123;pad:switch&#125;</a> <span class="about">Answers the next value of a rotating list on every call - striped rows without a counter.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">switch</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;odd&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-str">&#039;even&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">switch</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;red&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-str">&#039;green&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-str">&#039;blue&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/true">&#123;pad:true&#125;</a> <span class="about">The constant TRUE: its content renders once, as a level of its own.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">true</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">true</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/true</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="loops">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Loops and trees</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/branch">&#123;pad:branch&#125;</a> <span class="about">Inside a {tree}: renders its content only when the current row has children, its @else@ part for a leaf.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">branch</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">recurse</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/branch</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">branch</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/branch</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/break">&#123;pad:break&#125;</a> <span class="about">Leaves a loop at once: the rest of the current pass is dropped and no further row follows.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">break</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">break</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/cease">&#123;pad:cease&#125;</a> <span class="about">Ends a loop gracefully: the current pass finishes, and no further row follows.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">cease</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">cease</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/continue">&#123;pad:continue&#125;</a> <span class="about">Skips the rest of the current pass of a loop and goes on with its next row.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">continue</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">continue</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/recurse">&#123;pad:recurse&#125;</a> <span class="about">Inside a {tree}: renders the tree&#039;s body once more for the children of the current row.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">recurse</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/tree">&#123;pad:tree&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders a tree of rows: its body for every row, and {recurse} renders it again for the row&#039;s children.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">tree</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">recurse</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/tree</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">tree</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">children</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;items&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">branch</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">recurse</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/branch</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/tree</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">tree</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/tree</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/until">&#123;pad:until&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders its content again and again until a condition becomes true.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">until</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/until</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">until</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/until</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/while">&#123;pad:while&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders its content again and again for as long as a condition holds.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">while</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/while</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">while</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/while</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="values">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Variables and stores</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/at">&#123;pad:at&#125;</a> <span class="about">Looks a value up in a data set by a path of names and conditions - the @ notation as a tag.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">at</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;field@store&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">at</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;path.key=&#039;value&#039;.field@store&quot;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/bool">&#123;pad:bool&#125;</a> <span class="about">Reduces its content or a value to TRUE or FALSE and keeps it under a name, for a later {name} to test.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">bool</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> expression <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/bool</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">bool</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, value<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">name</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/name</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/content">&#123;pad:content&#125;</a> <span class="about">Keeps a piece of template under a name instead of printing it, for a later {name} to render.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">content</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/content</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">content</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">content</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/content</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/count">&#123;pad:count&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders its content only when a named data set or page array holds at least one element.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">count</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/count</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">count</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/count</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/data">&#123;pad:data&#125;</a> <span class="about">Turns its content - JSON, XML, YAML, CSV, a list or a range - into a named data set for a later tag to iterate.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">data</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/data</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">data</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">type</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;csv&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/data</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">data</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, source<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">data</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/data</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/decrement">&#123;pad:decrement&#125;</a> <span class="about">Takes one off a variable, starting it at -1 when it does not exist yet.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">decrement</span> <span class="hl-var">$name</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/holidays">&#123;pad:holidays&#125;</a> <span class="about">The public holidays of a country - seventeen of them, each a file of rules - as rows or a table, its regions too.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">holidays</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;nl&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">year</span>=<span class="hl-num">2026</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$date</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$name</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$local</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/holidays</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">holidays</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;de-by&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">year</span>=<span class="hl-num">2026</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">holidays</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;gb&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">region</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;sct&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">from</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;today&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">to</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;+6 months&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">lang</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;en&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/holidays</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/increment">&#123;pad:increment&#125;</a> <span class="about">Adds one to a variable, starting it at 1 when it does not exist yet.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">increment</span> <span class="hl-var">$name</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/set">&#123;pad:set&#125;</a> <span class="about">Assigns one or more variables, as globals that outlive the level the tag stands in.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">set</span> <span class="hl-var">$name</span> = value<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">set</span> <span class="hl-var">$name</span> = value, <span class="hl-var">$other</span> = value<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="database">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Database</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/array">&#123;pad:array&#125;</a> <span class="about">Runs a SQL query on the application database and repeats its content once per row.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">array</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;* from table where ...&quot;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/array</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">array</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;* from orders&quot;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;orders&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/array</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/check">&#123;pad:check&#125;</a> <span class="about">Asks the application database whether a row exists, and renders its content only when one does.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">check</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;table where ...&quot;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/check</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">check</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;users where email=&#039;x@y.z&#039;&quot;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/check</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/collection">&#123;pad:collection&#125;</a> <span class="about">Reads a folder of Markdown files from _content/ and repeats its content once per file, the front matter as fields.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">collection</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/collection</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">collection</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">slug</span>=<span class="hl-var">$slug</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/collection</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">collection</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, html<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/collection</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/field">&#123;pad:field&#125;</a> <span class="about">Runs a SQL query on the application database and prints the single value it answers.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">field</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;column from table where ...&quot;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">field</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;count(*) from orders&quot;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;orderCount&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/record">&#123;pad:record&#125;</a> <span class="about">Runs a SQL query on the application database and renders its content with the first row&#039;s columns as fields.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">record</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;* from table where ...&quot;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/record</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">record</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;* from users where id=5&quot;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;user&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/record</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/search">&#123;pad:search&#125;</a> <span class="about">Site search without a search server: the rows of a collection, a data store or a page&#039;s array a query finds, ranked by BM25, each with a marked excerpt.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">search</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;blog&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">q</span>=<span class="hl-var">$q</span>, <span class="hl-opt">fields</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;title^2, body&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">lang</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;en&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$title</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$score</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">!excerpt</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-var">@else@</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/search</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">search</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;products&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">q</span>=<span class="hl-var">$q</span>, <span class="hl-opt">words</span>=<span class="hl-num">20</span>, <span class="hl-opt">excerpt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;about&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/search</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">search</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;blog&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">q</span>=<span class="hl-var">$q</span>, <span class="hl-opt">link</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;url&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="pages">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Pages and execution</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/ajax">&#123;pad:ajax&#125;</a> <span class="about">Writes an empty div and a script that has the browser fetch another page into it after the page has loaded.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">ajax</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">ajax</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">fragment</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;fragmentName&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">ajax</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">app</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;other&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">ajax</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$colour</span> = <span class="hl-str">&#039;red&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/code">&#123;pad:code&#125;</a> <span class="about">Runs its content as PAD source in a separate engine pass and outputs what that pass produced.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">code</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/code</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">code</span> sandbox<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/code</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">code</span> reset<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/code</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">code</span> clean<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/code</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">code</span> <span class="hl-opt">function</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/code</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">echo</span> <span class="hl-var">$snippet</span> | code<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/get">&#123;pad:get&#125;</a> <span class="about">Fetches another page of the application over HTTP, as a second request, and inserts its output.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">get</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">get</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$colour</span> = <span class="hl-str">&#039;red&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/live">&#123;pad:live&#125;</a> <span class="about">A region of the page that re-renders on the server when something in it is clicked, submitted or changed, and swaps itself in.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">live</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-htm">&lt;button</span> <span class="hl-opt">pad-click</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;event&quot;</span> <span class="hl-opt">pad-value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;value&quot;</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>...<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/button</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/live</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">live</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-htm">&lt;form</span> <span class="hl-opt">pad-submit</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;event&quot;</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>...<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/form</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/live</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">live</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-htm">&lt;select</span> <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;field&quot;</span> <span class="hl-opt">pad-change</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;event&quot;</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>...<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/select</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/live</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pad">&#123;pad:pad&#125;</a> <span class="about">A tag with no behaviour of its own: its content renders once, or per row with data=, carrying the generic options.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;store&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad</span> <span class="hl-var">$name</span> = <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad</span> | upper<span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/page">&#123;pad:page&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders another page of the application - its PHP and its template - in place of the tag.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">page</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;dir/name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">page</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;products/42&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">page</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">include</span>=false<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">page</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, sandbox<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">page</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, reset<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">page</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, clean<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">page</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">function</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">page</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">app</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;other&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ajax</span>=false<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/restart">&#123;pad:restart&#125;</a> <span class="about">Abandons the page being built and renders another page in its place, in the same request.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">restart</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">restart</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$message</span> = <span class="hl-str">&#039;Saved&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sandbox">&#123;pad:sandbox&#125;</a> <span class="about">Runs its content as PAD source in an isolated pass that cannot see or leave behind anything of the page.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sandbox</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sandbox</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sandbox</span> <span class="hl-opt">function</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sandbox</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">echo</span> <span class="hl-var">$snippet</span> | sandbox<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="navigation">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Navigation</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pager">&#123;pad:pager&#125;</a> <span class="about">Page links for a tag written with the page option: first and last, a window round the current page, previous and next.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pager</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pager</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">window</span>=<span class="hl-num">2</span>, <span class="hl-opt">query</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;p&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pager</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$kind</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$page</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$label</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$href</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pager</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/redirect">&#123;pad:redirect&#125;</a> <span class="about">Sends the browser to another page or address with a 302 and ends the request.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">redirect</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">redirect</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;orders/list&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$status</span> = <span class="hl-str">&#039;open&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">redirect</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;https://example.com/&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sitemap">&#123;pad:sitemap&#125;</a> <span class="about">Lists the pages of the application, read from the file tree, as rows with page, url and lastmod.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sitemap</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$page</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$url</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$lastmod</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sitemap</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sitemap</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;dir&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sitemap</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="forms">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Forms and security</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/csrf">&#123;pad:csrf&#125;</a> <span class="about">The hidden form field holding the CSRF token of the visitor&#039;s session, or the bare token.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">csrf</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">csrf</span> token<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/flash">&#123;pad:flash&#125;</a> <span class="about">The flash messages of this request - set with padFlash on the page before - one occurrence each.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">flash</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$message</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$type</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/flash</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">flash</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/flash</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">flash</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;error&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$message</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/flash</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/form">&#123;pad:form&#125;</a> <span class="about">A form that posts back to the page, carrying the session&#039;s CSRF token and its own name, its fields refilling when it came back.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">form</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/form</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">form</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">method</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;get&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">class</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;search&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/form</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">form</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">error</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Please correct the errors below.&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/form</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">form</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, client, error<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/form</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/input">&#123;pad:input&#125;</a> <span class="about">A form field with its label that refills from what its form sent and shows the error validation found for it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">input</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">input</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;email&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">type</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;email&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;E-mail&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">id</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;mail&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">placeholder</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;you@example.org&#039;</span>, required<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">input</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;terms&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">type</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;checkbox&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;I agree&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;yes&#039;</span>, checked<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">input</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;email&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">type</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;email&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;E-mail&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rules</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;required|email&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">input:slider</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;volume&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Volume&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">min</span>=<span class="hl-num">0</span>, <span class="hl-opt">max</span>=<span class="hl-num">100</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/nonce">&#123;pad:nonce&#125;</a> <span class="about">This request&#039;s Content-Security-Policy nonce, for a script or style the policy lets run.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">nonce</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/textarea">&#123;pad:textarea&#125;</a> <span class="about">A text area with its label that refills from what its form sent and shows the error validation found for it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">textarea</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;message&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">textarea</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;message&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Message&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">id</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;msg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">6</span>, required<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">textarea</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;message&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Message&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">6</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rules</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;required|max:2000&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/validator">&#123;pad:validator&#125;</a> <span class="about">The browser&#039;s checker of the validation rules as one inline script, for a form a component draws.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">validator</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
