<section class="section" id="access">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Users and access</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/auth">&#123;pad:auth&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders its content when a user is logged in, with the user&#039;s row as its data, and its else half for a guest.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">auth</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/auth</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">auth</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> Hello <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$name</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">else</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/auth</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">auth</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/auth</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/can">&#123;pad:can&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders its content when the user may do something a gate decides, and its else half when not.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">can</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;ability&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/can</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">can</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;ability&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$value</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">else</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/can</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">rows</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">can</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;ability&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$rows</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/can</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/rows</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/cannot">&#123;pad:cannot&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders its content when the user may not do something a gate decides - can turned round.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">cannot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;ability&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$value</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/cannot</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">cannot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;ability&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$value</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">else</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/cannot</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/feature">&#123;pad:feature&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders its content while a feature flag is on for this visitor, and its else half while it is off.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">feature</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;flag&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/feature</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">feature</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;flag&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">else</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/feature</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/guest">&#123;pad:guest&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders its content when nobody is logged in, and its else half for a user.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">guest</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/guest</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">guest</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">else</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/guest</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/notifications">&#123;pad:notifications&#125;</a> <span class="about">Lists the notifications padNotify kept on the site for the user logged in, unread, each with a link that marks it read.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">notifications</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">notifications</span> <span class="hl-opt">for</span>=<span class="hl-var">$id</span>, all<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$title</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$text</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$created</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$link</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/notifications</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/oauth">&#123;pad:oauth&#125;</a> <span class="about">Writes a &quot;Log in with ...&quot; link styled as a button, for the OAuth login of padOAuthStart and padOAuthUser.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;github&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;oidc&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;?login/company&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Log in with your company account&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="files">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Files</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/dir">&#123;pad:dir&#125;</a> <span class="about">Lists the names in a directory, one occurrence each - the plain version of files.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">dir</span> <span class="hl-var">$path</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$dir</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/dir</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/exists">&#123;pad:exists&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders its content when a file or directory is there, its else half when not.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">exists</span> <span class="hl-var">$path</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/exists</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">exists</span> <span class="hl-var">$path</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/exists</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/file">&#123;pad:file&#125;</a> <span class="about">Writes its rendered content to a file under DATA/ instead of to the page.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">file</span> <span class="hl-opt">dir</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;exports&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;orders&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ext</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;csv&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/file</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">file</span> <span class="hl-opt">dir</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;reports&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;daily&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ext</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;html&#039;</span>, date, stamp, <span class="hl-opt">id</span>=<span class="hl-num">8</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/file</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/files">&#123;pad:files&#125;</a> <span class="about">Scans a directory and iterates its entries, with path, file, ext, item and dir for each.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">files</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;dir&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">base</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;app&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$item</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$file</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$ext</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/files</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">files</span> <span class="hl-opt">dir</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;dir&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">base</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;data&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">mask</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;*.txt&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">exclude</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;old*&#039;</span>, recursive, onlyFiles, includeHidden<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/files</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">files</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;dir&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">base</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;app&#039;</span>, onlyDirs<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/files</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="network">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Network and mail</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/ai">&#123;pad:ai&#125;</a> <span class="about">Prints the answer of a language model to the prompt its content renders, as text, kept for a while.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">ai</span> <span class="hl-opt">provider</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;anthropic&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">model</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">system</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">tokens</span>=<span class="hl-num">500</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ttl</span>=<span class="hl-num">86400</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/ai</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">ai</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;prompt&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/curl">&#123;pad:curl&#125;</a> <span class="about">Fetches a URL and answers the body of the response, optionally kept in a cache for a number of seconds.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">curl</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;https://example.com/data.json&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">curl</span> <span class="hl-opt">url</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;https://example.com/data.json&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ttl</span>=<span class="hl-num">600</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">curl</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;https://example.com/api&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">post</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;a=1&amp;b=2&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">user</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">password</span>=<span class="hl-var">$secret</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">curl</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;SELF://shop/?orders&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$status</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;open&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">curl</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;https://example.com/api&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">get</span>=<span class="hl-var">$query</span>, <span class="hl-opt">cookies</span>=<span class="hl-var">$jar</span>, <span class="hl-opt">headers</span>=<span class="hl-var">$headers</span>, <span class="hl-opt">options</span>=<span class="hl-var">$curlOptions</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/mail">&#123;pad:mail&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders a template, or its own content, to an e-mail with an HTML and a text part, and sends it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">mail</span> <span class="hl-opt">to</span>=<span class="hl-var">$email</span>, <span class="hl-opt">template</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">subject</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Order confirmation&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">mail</span> <span class="hl-opt">to</span>=<span class="hl-var">$email</span>, <span class="hl-opt">subject</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Welcome&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">from</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;shop@example.com&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">cc</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;a@example.com&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">bcc</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;b@example.com&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">replyTo</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;help@example.com&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/mail</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="output">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Output control</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/asset">&#123;pad:asset&#125;</a> <span class="about">The address of a file of www/&lt;app&gt;/ with a version of its contents, safe behind a far-future cache header.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">asset</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;file&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">asset</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;file&#039;</span>, tag<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/cache">&#123;pad:cache&#125;</a> <span class="about">Keeps a named section of the page rendered for a number of seconds - the fragment cache.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">cache</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/cache</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">cache</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ttl</span>=<span class="hl-num">300</span>, <span class="hl-opt">vary</span>=<span class="hl-var">$value</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/cache</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">anytag</span> <span class="hl-opt">cache</span>=<span class="hl-num">3600</span>, <span class="hl-opt">vary</span>=<span class="hl-var">$value</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/anytag</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/close">&#123;pad:close&#125;</a> <span class="about">Writes a literal closing brace that the template engine does not read as the end of a tag.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">close</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/echo">&#123;pad:echo&#125;</a> <span class="about">Evaluates an expression and prints the result - the general output tag, and the one to put in front of a pipe.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">echo</span> expression<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">echo</span> expression | pipe | pipe<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/flush">&#123;pad:flush&#125;</a> <span class="about">Sends the page rendered so far to the browser at once, so it can load the stylesheets while a slow part below renders.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">flush</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/foo">&#123;pad:foo&#125;</a> <span class="about">A built-in demo tag that answers a fixed line, there to show how the type prefixes resolve a name.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">foo</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:foo</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/ignore">&#123;pad:ignore&#125;</a> <span class="about">Keeps PAD from reading the braces in its content as tags - for inline JavaScript, CSS, JSON and code samples.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">ignore</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/ignore</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/nocache">&#123;pad:nocache&#125;</a> <span class="about">Marks a part of a cached page or {cache} section that renders again on every request, the hit included.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">nocache</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/nocache</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">cache</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ttl</span>=<span class="hl-num">300</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">nocache</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/nocache</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/cache</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/open">&#123;pad:open&#125;</a> <span class="about">Writes a literal opening brace that the template engine does not read as the start of a tag.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">open</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/output">&#123;pad:output&#125;</a> <span class="about">Switches how the running request answers - a web page, the console, a file, a download, JSON or CSV.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">output</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;type&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/reactData">&#123;pad:reactData&#125;</a> <span class="about">Writes the mount point of a React component, its data from a provider in _providers/ as JSON in an attribute.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">reactData</span> <span class="hl-opt">id</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;products&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">reactData</span> <span class="hl-opt">id</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;products&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">provider</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;catalog&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">type</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;array&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/spaceless">&#123;pad:spaceless&#125;</a> <span class="about">Takes out the whitespace between HTML tags, and at both ends of the content.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">spaceless</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/spaceless</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/tidy">&#123;pad:tidy&#125;</a> <span class="about">Rewrites the HTML it wraps as tidied, indented markup through PHP&#039;s tidy extension.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">tidy</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/tidy</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/trans">&#123;pad:trans&#125;</a> <span class="about">Writes the text a key has in the _lang/ catalog of the request&#039;s locale, with plural forms and substitutions.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">trans</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;key&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">trans</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;key&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">count</span>=<span class="hl-var">$n</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">trans</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;key&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-var">$value</span>, <span class="hl-opt">other</span>=<span class="hl-var">$value</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/vite">&#123;pad:vite&#125;</a> <span class="about">Links the scripts and stylesheets of a Vite build by the name of their source - or the dev server while it runs.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">vite</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;src/main.js&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">vite</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;src/main.jsx&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-str">&#039;src/admin.js&#039;</span>, react<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="text">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Text and formatting</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/attrs">&#123;pad:attrs&#125;</a> <span class="about">Writes HTML attributes from values - quoted and escaped, a true boolean attribute bare, a false one left out.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">attrs</span> <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-var">$value</span>, <span class="hl-opt">other</span>=<span class="hl-var">$value</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">attrs</span> required, <span class="hl-opt">aria-expanded</span>=<span class="hl-var">$open</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">attrs</span> <span class="hl-var">$array</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/classes">&#123;pad:classes&#125;</a> <span class="about">Builds a class list from fixed names and names that are in only while their condition holds.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">classes</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;fixed names&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=condition, <span class="hl-opt">other</span>=condition<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">classes</span> <span class="hl-var">$array</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/countdown">&#123;pad:countdown&#125;</a> <span class="about">The time left until a moment, in words inside a time element - ticking every second in the browser when asked.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">countdown</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;2026-12-31 00:00&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">countdown</span> <span class="hl-var">$moment</span>, <span class="hl-opt">units</span>=<span class="hl-num">3</span>, <span class="hl-opt">past</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;We are live!&#039;</span>, live<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">countdown</span> <span class="hl-var">$moment</span>, <span class="hl-opt">now</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;2026-10-08 12:00&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">format</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;j M Y&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diff">&#123;pad:diff&#125;</a> <span class="about">Shows what changed between two texts - word by word in the flow of the text, or line by line in one column or side by side.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diff</span> <span class="hl-var">$old</span>, <span class="hl-var">$new</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diff</span> <span class="hl-var">$old</span>, <span class="hl-var">$new</span>, lines, <span class="hl-opt">from</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Monday&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">to</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Friday&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diff</span> <span class="hl-var">$old</span>, <span class="hl-var">$new</span>, side, <span class="hl-opt">from</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;before&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">to</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;after&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/emoji">&#123;pad:emoji&#125;</a> <span class="about">An emoji by its GitHub or Slack shortcode, as a labelled image a screen reader names.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">emoji</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;rocket&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">emoji</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;:tada:&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Party!&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/excerpt">&#123;pad:excerpt&#125;</a> <span class="about">Cuts a long text to whole words with an ellipsis, centred on the first match of search words, every match marked.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">excerpt</span> <span class="hl-var">$text</span>, <span class="hl-opt">words</span>=<span class="hl-num">40</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">excerpt</span> <span class="hl-var">$text</span>, <span class="hl-opt">words</span>=<span class="hl-num">24</span>, <span class="hl-opt">highlight</span>=<span class="hl-var">$q</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ellipsis</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;...&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">excerpt</span> <span class="hl-var">$html</span>, <span class="hl-opt">words</span>=<span class="hl-num">30</span>, html<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/fake">&#123;pad:fake&#125;</a> <span class="about">Makes a fake value - a name, an email, a price, an IBAN, 27 kinds - or rows of several kinds, the same on every request, for prototypes and previews.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">fake</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;kind&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">fake:kind</span> <span class="hl-opt">from</span>=<span class="hl-num">1</span>, <span class="hl-opt">to</span>=<span class="hl-num">6</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">fake</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name, email&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">5</span>, <span class="hl-opt">toData</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;store&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">fake</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;field:kind, field:kind&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">3</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/fake</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/highlight">&#123;pad:highlight&#125;</a> <span class="about">Colours source code on the server - PAD, PHP, HTML, CSS, JavaScript, JSON, YAML, SQL or shell - with optional line numbers and marked lines.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">highlight</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;lang&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/highlight</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">highlight</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;lang&#039;</span>, lines, <span class="hl-opt">mark</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;3, 5-7&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/highlight</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">highlight</span> <span class="hl-opt">file</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;_data/products.json&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/lorem">&#123;pad:lorem&#125;</a> <span class="about">Placeholder text - Lorem ipsum and Latin words - in words, sentences or paragraphs, the same text for the same seed.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">lorem</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">lorem</span> <span class="hl-opt">words</span>=<span class="hl-num">4</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">lorem</span> <span class="hl-opt">sentences</span>=<span class="hl-num">2</span>, <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">lorem</span> <span class="hl-opt">paragraphs</span>=<span class="hl-num">3</span>, <span class="hl-opt">sentences</span>=<span class="hl-num">4</span>, varied<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/markdown">&#123;pad:markdown&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders its content, or the value of its parameter, as Markdown and writes the HTML.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">markdown</span> <span class="hl-var">$text</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">markdown</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/markdown</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">markdown</span> html<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/markdown</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">markdown</span> ignore<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/markdown</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/source">&#123;pad:source&#125;</a> <span class="about">Shows the files behind a page as tabs, each coloured as highlight colours it - the code shown is the code that runs.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">source</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">source</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;page&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">files</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;_lib/orders.php, www:orders.js&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">source</span> <span class="hl-opt">only</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;_tags/card.pad, www:card.js&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/timeago">&#123;pad:timeago&#125;</a> <span class="about">How long ago a moment was, in words, inside a time element that keeps the exact moment as its datetime and title.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">timeago</span> <span class="hl-var">$moment</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">timeago</span> <span class="hl-var">$moment</span>, <span class="hl-opt">now</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;2026-10-08 12:00&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">format</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;j M Y&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/toc">&#123;pad:toc&#125;</a> <span class="about">A table of contents of the page from its own rendered headings, nested and linked - filled in once the page has rendered.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toc</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toc</span> <span class="hl-opt">levels</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;2-4&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;On this page&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">list</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;ul&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="graphics">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Charts and codes</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode">&#123;pad:barcode&#125;</a> <span class="about">Draws a barcode as inline SVG, encoded on the server - 13 symbologies, from EAN-13 and Code 128 to QR and Data Matrix.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;number&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode</span> <span class="hl-var">$code</span>, <span class="hl-opt">type</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;code128&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">height</span>=<span class="hl-num">60</span>, <span class="hl-opt">scale</span>=<span class="hl-num">2</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span>, plain, <span class="hl-opt">color</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#000&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">background</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#fff&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:ean13</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;number&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">code39</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;TEXT&#039;</span>, check<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode</span> <span class="hl-var">$text</span>, <span class="hl-opt">type</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;datamatrix&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">size</span>=<span class="hl-num">160</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/calendar">&#123;pad:calendar&#125;</a> <span class="about">Writes a month as a calendar table with its events, or hands over its weeks and days as rows for markup of your own.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">calendar</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">calendar</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;2026-10&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">date</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">link</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">query</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;month&#039;</span>, sunday<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">calendar</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;2026-10&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">holidays</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;nl&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">calendar</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;2026-10&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">days</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">events</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/events</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/days</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/calendar</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart">&#123;pad:chart&#125;</a> <span class="about">Draws a chart as inline SVG on the server - 45 kinds, from bars and lines to sankeys, maps of a hierarchy and org charts.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;bar&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field, field&#039;</span>, stacked, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">600</span>, <span class="hl-opt">height</span>=<span class="hl-num">300</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram">&#123;pad:diagram&#125;</a> <span class="about">Draws a diagram from Mermaid-style lines of text, laid out on the server as inline SVG - 9 kinds, from flowcharts and sequence diagrams to state machines, class and entity-relationship diagrams, mind maps, trees, user journeys and commit graphs.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram</span> <span class="hl-opt">direction</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;LR&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">edges</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;curved&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> A[Start] --<span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span> B<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">Ready</span>?<span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/map">&#123;pad:map&#125;</a> <span class="about">Draws a choropleth map as inline SVG - every country coloured by a value of its row - of the world or one of its 22 regions.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">map</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;world&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">key</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">scale</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;quantile&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">720</span>, <span class="hl-opt">height</span>=<span class="hl-num">400</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/notation">&#123;pad:notation&#125;</a> <span class="about">A text written in a notation, drawn on the server - 12 of them, from formulas in AsciiMath and chemical equations to a tune in ABC, guitar chords, braille, morse, signal flags and chess, go and draughts positions.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">notation</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;kind&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span>, ...options<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/palette">&#123;pad:palette&#125;</a> <span class="about">The colours of a palette as swatches with their hex values - 18 of them: viridis and its family, the colour-blind safe Okabe-Ito and Tol schemes, ColorBrewer&#039;s scales - the same palettes palette= gives a chart.</span>

    <div class="cheat-item">

    <div class="cheat-item">

<section class="section" id="pictures">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Pictures and media</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/avatar">&#123;pad:avatar&#125;</a> <span class="about">Draws an initials avatar as inline SVG - the letters of a name in a circle coloured by a hash of it, no image needed.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">avatar</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">avatar</span> <span class="hl-var">$name</span>, <span class="hl-opt">size</span>=<span class="hl-num">48</span>, <span class="hl-opt">shape</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;square&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/country">&#123;pad:country&#125;</a> <span class="about">Writes a country&#039;s flag from its ISO 3166 code or English name, optionally with the name beside it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">country</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;NL&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">country</span> <span class="hl-var">$code</span>, name<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/embed">&#123;pad:embed&#125;</a> <span class="about">Embeds a video, a sound, a map, a pen or a document of another site behind a click-to-load facade - 12 providers, nothing of theirs loaded before the click.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">embed</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=id&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">embed:vimeo</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;id&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">at</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;1:30&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ratio</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;4:3&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">openstreetmap</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;latitude,longitude&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">zoom</span>=<span class="hl-num">15</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">embed</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;https://codepen.io/user/pen/id&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">tab</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;html,result&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">audio</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;sounds/bell.mp3&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/gravatar">&#123;pad:gravatar&#125;</a> <span class="about">Writes the Gravatar picture of an e-mail address as an img - only a hash of the address leaves the page - or a local avatar instead.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">gravatar</span> <span class="hl-var">$email</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>

<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">gravatar</span> <span class="hl-var">$email</span>, <span class="hl-opt">fallback</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;avatar&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/icon">&#123;pad:icon&#125;</a> <span class="about">Writes an interface icon from PAD&#039;s own set of 202 as inline SVG, drawn in the colour of the text around it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">icon</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;arrow-right&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">icon</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;trash&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">size</span>=<span class="hl-num">20</span>, <span class="hl-opt">stroke</span>=<span class="hl-num">2</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">icon:star</span> <span class="hl-opt">size</span>=<span class="hl-num">20</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/identicon">&#123;pad:identicon&#125;</a> <span class="about">Draws a GitHub-style identicon as inline SVG - a symmetric 5 by 5 pattern and a colour from a hash of the value.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">identicon</span> <span class="hl-var">$email</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">identicon</span> <span class="hl-var">$value</span>, <span class="hl-opt">size</span>=<span class="hl-num">64</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/img">&#123;pad:img&#125;</a> <span class="about">Writes a picture of the application&#039;s www directory as a thumbnail of the size the page shows it at, resized once with GD.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">img</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photos/harbour.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">400</span>, <span class="hl-opt">height</span>=<span class="hl-num">300</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">img</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photos/dunes.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">300</span>, <span class="hl-opt">height</span>=<span class="hl-num">300</span>, <span class="hl-opt">fit</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;contain&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">format</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;webp&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">quality</span>=<span class="hl-num">70</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">img</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photos/harbour.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">400</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;duotone&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">colors</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#123, #fc6&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">img</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photos/harbour.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">400</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;grayscale, dither&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">img</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photos/harbour.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">400</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;blurhash&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern">&#123;pad:pattern&#125;</a> <span class="about">Draws a seeded SVG background or a piece of generative art on the server - 16 kinds, from stripes and dots to contour lines and circle packing - the same bytes for the same options.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;topography&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">960</span>, <span class="hl-opt">height</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;dots&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">size</span>=<span class="hl-num">24</span>, <span class="hl-opt">colors</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#fdfbf7, #e0c9a6&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:voronoi</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">3</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Shards of colour&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-htm">&lt;div</span> <span class="hl-opt">style</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;background-image:{pattern &#039;waves&#039;, as=&#039;url&#039;/}&quot;</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>...<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/div</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pdf">&#123;pad:pdf&#125;</a> <span class="about">Sends what its content renders as a PDF instead of the page - a writer in plain PHP, the standard fonts, no library.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pdf</span> <span class="hl-opt">file</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">size</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A4&#039;</span>, landscape, <span class="hl-opt">font</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;helvetica&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pdf</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pdf</span> preview, <span class="hl-opt">file</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pdf</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pdf</span> download<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/placeholder">&#123;pad:placeholder&#125;</a> <span class="about">Draws a grey image placeholder as inline SVG - crossing lines and the size or a text in the middle.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">placeholder</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;600x300&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">text</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">placeholder</span> <span class="hl-opt">ratio</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;16:9&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">640</span>, fluid<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/printable">&#123;pad:printable&#125;</a> <span class="about">Pages made for paper - 10 kinds, from sheets of labels and name badges to a letter for a window envelope - the content between the tags one item, repeated over the data and laid out on sheets that print as they show, and come out of the PDF writer alike.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">printable</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, ...<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/printable</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">printable:labels</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;products&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">sheet</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;a4-3x8&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;b</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$name</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;/b</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/printable:labels</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">printable:tickets</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;seats&#039;</span>, pdf<span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;h2</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$show</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;/h2</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;aside</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">qr</span> <span class="hl-var">$code</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;/aside</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/printable:tickets</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">printable:letter</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;customers&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">sender</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;PAD, Utrecht&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;address</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$name</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;/address</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;p</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>...<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/p</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/printable:letter</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/progress">&#123;pad:progress&#125;</a> <span class="about">Writes an accessible progress bar - the native progress element, styled, with its label and percentage - or a row of step dots.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">progress</span> <span class="hl-num">72</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">progress</span> <span class="hl-var">$done</span>, <span class="hl-opt">max</span>=<span class="hl-num">100</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">color</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;success&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">size</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;small&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">progress</span> <span class="hl-num">3</span>, <span class="hl-opt">max</span>=<span class="hl-num">5</span>, steps, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/rating">&#123;pad:rating&#125;</a> <span class="about">Draws a score as stars or hearts in inline SVG - whole ones filled, a part of one clipped - and says it in words.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">rating</span> <span class="hl-num">4.5</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">rating</span> <span class="hl-var">$score</span>, <span class="hl-opt">max</span>=<span class="hl-num">5</span>, <span class="hl-opt">size</span>=<span class="hl-num">20</span>, <span class="hl-opt">icon</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;heart&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound">&#123;pad:sound&#125;</a> <span class="about">A short sound made from a recipe and played by the browser - 17 of them, from a chime and a pop to a coin and a drum kit - a button, a binding to elements of the page, or a WAV file.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>

<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:chime</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:success</span> <span class="hl-opt">on</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;click&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">target</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#save&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:bell</span> download<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/video">&#123;pad:video&#125;</a> <span class="about">Shows a YouTube or Vimeo video as its poster with a play button, loading the player only when pressed - or a video file as a video element.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">video</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=id&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">video</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;https://vimeo.com/id#t=1m30s&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">poster</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;photos/still.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ratio</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;16:9&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>

<section class="section" id="widgets">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Widgets</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/copy">&#123;pad:copy&#125;</a> <span class="about">A text in a code block with a button that copies it to the clipboard.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">copy</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">copy</span> <span class="hl-var">$value</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Copy command&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">copy</span> <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Copy&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/copy</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/datatable">&#123;pad:datatable&#125;</a> <span class="about">Rows as a complete HTML table - formatted numbers, a totals row, sorting and paging on the server.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">datatable</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">columns</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;a, b, c&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">labels</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A, B, C&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">totals</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;c&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">datatable</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">format</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;c:currency(&#039;EUR&#039;)&quot;</span>, sortable, striped, <span class="hl-opt">caption</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Caption&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">datatable</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span>, <span class="hl-opt">query</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;prefix_&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">datatable</span> <span class="hl-opt">totals</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;amount&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> month,amount ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/datatable</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect">&#123;pad:effect&#125;</a> <span class="about">Motion and decoration on a text or a block, CSS first - 13 of them, from a typewriter, a glitch and a marker stroke to a scroll reveal, a marquee, loaders and confetti.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, ...<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:name</span> ...<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:name</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">typewriter</span> <span class="hl-opt">speed</span>=<span class="hl-num">14</span>, <span class="hl-opt">delay</span>=<span class="hl-num">0</span>, <span class="hl-opt">caret</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;bar&#039;</span>, mono<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/typewriter</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">glitch</span> <span class="hl-opt">repeat</span>=<span class="hl-num">3</span>, <span class="hl-opt">duration</span>=<span class="hl-num">2.5</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/glitch</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">shimmer</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/shimmer</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">gradient</span> <span class="hl-opt">colors</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#f97316, #db2777&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">angle</span>=<span class="hl-num">90</span>, animate<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/gradient</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">wave</span> <span class="hl-opt">height</span>=<span class="hl-num">0.25</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/wave</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">highlighter</span> <span class="hl-opt">color</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;gold&#039;</span>, scroll<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/highlighter</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">countup</span> <span class="hl-opt">to</span>=<span class="hl-num">1250</span>, <span class="hl-opt">from</span>=<span class="hl-num">0</span>, <span class="hl-opt">prefix</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;€&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">suffix</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;+&#039;</span>, scroll<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">marquee</span> <span class="hl-opt">duration</span>=<span class="hl-num">20</span>, <span class="hl-opt">direction</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;left&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">gap</span>=<span class="hl-num">48</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/marquee</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">reveal</span> <span class="hl-opt">motion</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;up&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">distance</span>=<span class="hl-num">32</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/reveal</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">tilt</span> <span class="hl-opt">angle</span>=<span class="hl-num">8</span>, <span class="hl-opt">toward</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;top-right&#039;</span>, glare<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/tilt</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
