<section class="section" id="layout">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Layouts and components</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/block">&#123;pad:block&#125;</a> <span class="about">A named region of a layout or wrapper - and a page&#039;s override of it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">block</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> default content <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/block</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">block</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">parent</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span> more content <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/block</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/define">&#123;pad:define&#125;</a> <span class="about">Writes a custom tag in the template itself - what a _tags/name.pad file is, without the file.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">define</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">#parm</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@content@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/define</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">define</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">parms</span> title, <span class="hl-opt">tone</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;info&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">slot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;footer&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-var">@content@</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/slot</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/define</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">name</span> <span class="hl-opt">parm</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">name</span> <span class="hl-opt">parm</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> content <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">slot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;footer&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>fill<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/slot</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/name</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">define:name</span> ...<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/extends">&#123;pad:extends&#125;</a> <span class="about">Frames the page with a layout instead of its directories&#039; wrappers.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">extends</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;layout&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">extends</span> <span class="hl-var">$layout</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/fragment">&#123;pad:fragment&#125;</a> <span class="about">A named part of the page that a request can ask for alone - for htmx and ajax swaps.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">fragment</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/fragment</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/macro">&#123;pad:macro&#125;</a> <span class="about">A template function: declared parameters, given in order or by name, read as fields inside its body.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">macro</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, required, <span class="hl-opt">optional</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;default&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$required</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$optional</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/macro</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">name</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">name</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-str">&#039;other&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">name</span> <span class="hl-opt">required</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">optional</span>=<span class="hl-var">$x</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">name</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> content for <span class="hl-var">@content@</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/name</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">macro:name</span> ...<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/meta">&#123;pad:meta&#125;</a> <span class="about">The page&#039;s metadata - a title, a layout, a cache time or any value - read while the page is assembled.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">meta</span> <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Title&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=value<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">meta</span> <span class="hl-opt">layout</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;layout&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">cache</span>=<span class="hl-num">600</span>, <span class="hl-opt">access</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;admin&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">sitemap</span>=false<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/parent">&#123;pad:parent&#125;</a> <span class="about">Inside an overriding block: the content of the block it overrides.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">block</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">parent</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/block</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/parms">&#123;pad:parms&#125;</a> <span class="about">Declares the parameters of a custom tag, with defaults, at the top of its template.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">parms</span> name<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">parms</span> title, <span class="hl-opt">subtitle</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">tone</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;info&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/push">&#123;pad:push&#125;</a> <span class="about">Renders its content and adds it to a named stack, for a stack tag elsewhere in the page to print.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">push</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/push</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">push</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">once</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;key&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/push</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">push</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, once<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/push</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/section">&#123;pad:section&#125;</a> <span class="about">A block of a landing page - 15 of them, from a hero, the features and the prices to the questions, a newsletter form and the footer - in pure CSS.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;kind&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-str">&#039;heading&#039;</span>, ...<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/section</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:kind</span> <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;heading&#039;</span>, ...<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/section:kind</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:hero</span> <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;heading&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">button</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">link</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;?page&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">image</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;picture.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> text <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/section:hero</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:features</span> <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;heading&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">columns</span>=<span class="hl-num">3</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:item</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">icon</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;zap&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> text <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/section:item</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/section:features</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:pricing</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;plans&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:pricing</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:item</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">price</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;€12&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">period</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;month&#039;</span>, featured<span class="hl-brc">}</span> a feature a line <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/section:item</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/section:pricing</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:faq</span> <span class="hl-opt">open</span>=<span class="hl-num">1</span>, single<span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:item</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;question&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> answer <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/section:item</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/section:faq</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:cta</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;heading&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">button</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">link</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;?page&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:newsletter</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;heading&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">button</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Subscribe&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:contact</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;heading&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">mail</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;...&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">phone</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;...&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">address</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;...&#039;</span>, form<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:footer</span> <span class="hl-opt">brand</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">note</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;small print&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:item</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;column&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> Label | <span class="hl-var">?page</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/section:item</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/section:footer</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:hero</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">slot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;eyebrow&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/slot</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">slot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;actions&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/slot</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/section:hero</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/shadow">&#123;pad:shadow&#125;</a> <span class="about">The shadow root of a custom element, rendered on the server as declarative shadow DOM.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">shadow</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/shadow</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">shadow</span> <span class="hl-opt">css</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;file.css, www:other.css&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">mode</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;closed&#039;</span>, focus<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/shadow</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/slot">&#123;pad:slot&#125;</a> <span class="about">A named place in a custom tag&#039;s template - and the fill a caller gives it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">slot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> default content <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/slot</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">slot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-htm">&lt;footer</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-var">@content@</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;/footer</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/slot</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">slot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/stack">&#123;pad:stack&#125;</a> <span class="about">Prints everything pushed to a named stack, from above or below it in the page.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">stack</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/theme">&#123;pad:theme&#125;</a> <span class="about">The colours of the page swapped for a theme - 10 of them, from paper and sepia to a terminal, an ocean and a high-contrast one - or its content alone in a box of the theme.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">theme</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">theme</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;paper&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">theme</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;terminal&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/theme</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-var">$padTheme</span> = <span class="hl-str">&#039;paper&#039;</span>;</code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/themes">&#123;pad:themes&#125;</a> <span class="about">A switcher of the themes: the visitor&#039;s choice kept in a cookie of the application, its pages in that theme from then on - a form, no JavaScript needed.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">themes</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">themes</span> <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Colours&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">button</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Use&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>

<section class="section" id="debug">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Debugging and errors</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/assert">&#123;pad:assert&#125;</a> <span class="about">A condition a test run holds the page to; silent everywhere else.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">assert</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">assert</span> condition, <span class="hl-str">&#039;message&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/debug">&#123;pad:debug&#125;</a> <span class="about">Shows values as a collapsible tree in the page, for a local request only, while rendering goes on.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">debug</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">debug</span> <span class="hl-var">$value</span>, <span class="hl-var">$other</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">debug</span> <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-var">$value</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/dump">&#123;pad:dump&#125;</a> <span class="about">Stops the request and shows the debug dump of the engine&#039;s state at that point.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">dump</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/error">&#123;pad:error&#125;</a> <span class="about">Raises a PAD error from the template, handled as the configured error action says.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">error</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;message&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/exception">&#123;pad:exception&#125;</a> <span class="about">Throws a real PHP exception from the template, to exercise the error handling.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">exception</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;message&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/exit">&#123;pad:exit&#125;</a> <span class="about">Ends the request at once, with an empty body - the PAD way to stop instead of PHP&#039;s exit.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">exit</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/trace">&#123;pad:trace&#125;</a> <span class="about">Switches the execution trace on for just the part of the page it wraps.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">trace</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/trace</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="sequences">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Sequences</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/action">&#123;pad:action&#125;</a> <span class="about">Runs one sequence action - reverse, sum, first and the rest - over a store, a range or a type, and iterates the result.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">action</span> store, reverse<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/action</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">action</span> reverse, store<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/action</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">action</span> store, <span class="hl-opt">first</span>=<span class="hl-num">2</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/action</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">action</span> sum, <span class="hl-str">&#039;31..40&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/action</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">action:reverse</span> store<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/action:reverse</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">action:shift</span> store, <span class="hl-num">2</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/action:shift</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/flag">&#123;pad:flag&#125;</a> <span class="about">Replaces every value of a sequence with 1 or 0: whether it belongs to a sequence type.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">flag</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;1..10&#039;</span>, even<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/flag</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">flag</span> prime, <span class="hl-opt">from</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">12</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/flag</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">flag:prime</span> <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/flag:prime</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">flag</span> store, prime<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/flag</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/keep">&#123;pad:keep&#125;</a> <span class="about">Keeps only the values of a sequence that belong to a sequence type.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">keep</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;1..50&#039;</span>, square<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/keep</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">keep</span> palindrome, <span class="hl-opt">from</span>=<span class="hl-num">100</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">5</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/keep</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">keep:prime</span> <span class="hl-opt">from</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">4</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/keep:prime</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">keep</span> store, even<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/keep</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/make">&#123;pad:make&#125;</a> <span class="about">Replaces every value of a sequence with what a sequence type makes of it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">make</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;1..5&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">add</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/make</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">make</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;1..5&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">add</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span>, <span class="hl-opt">multiply</span>=<span class="hl-num">2</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/make</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">make</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;1..6&#039;</span>, prime<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/make</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">make:fibonacci</span> <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">8</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/make:fibonacci</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">make</span> store, square<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/make</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pull">&#123;pad:pull&#125;</a> <span class="about">Iterates a stored sequence, optionally through plays and actions.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pull</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;store&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pull</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pull</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pull</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pull:store</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pull:store</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pull</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;store&#039;</span>, reverse, <span class="hl-opt">first</span>=<span class="hl-num">3</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pull</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pull</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;store&#039;</span>, keep, prime<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pull</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/remove">&#123;pad:remove&#125;</a> <span class="about">Removes the values of a sequence that belong to a sequence type - the inverse of keep.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">remove</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;1..10&#039;</span>, prime<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/remove</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">remove</span> prime, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">5</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/remove</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">remove:odd</span> <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">5</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/remove:odd</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">remove</span> store, even<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/remove</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/resume">&#123;pad:resume&#125;</a> <span class="about">Transforms the sequence pushed last, in place, through an action or a play.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">resume</span> reverse<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">resume</span> <span class="hl-opt">add</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">resume</span> keep, prime<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">resume</span> <span class="hl-opt">first</span>=<span class="hl-num">3</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sequence">&#123;pad:sequence&#125;</a> <span class="about">Generates a range, a list or one of 80 mathematical sequences, and iterates its values.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;1..10&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence</span> <span class="hl-opt">range</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;1..100&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">increment</span>=<span class="hl-num">5</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence</span> <span class="hl-opt">list</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;3;1;2&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence</span> prime, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;p&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence</span> fibonacci, <span class="hl-opt">from</span>=<span class="hl-num">1</span>, <span class="hl-opt">to</span>=<span class="hl-num">20</span>, <span class="hl-opt">stop</span>=<span class="hl-num">100</span>, <span class="hl-opt">skip</span>=<span class="hl-num">2</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence</span> random, <span class="hl-opt">minimal</span>=<span class="hl-num">1</span>, <span class="hl-opt">maximal</span>=<span class="hl-num">100</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">5</span>, unique, randomly<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;1..20&#039;</span>, keep, prime, sum<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;1..20&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">keep</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;prime&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">action</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;first|3&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence</span> prime, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span>, <span class="hl-opt">push</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;primes&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">toData</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;primeList&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence</span> <span class="hl-opt">pull</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;primes&#039;</span>, reverse<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="kind-chart">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Chart types</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/arc">&#123;chart:arc&#125;</a> <span class="about">Nodes on a line in the order they first appear, a dot as large as its number of links, its name under it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:arc</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">arc</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/area">&#123;chart:area&#125;</a> <span class="about">Filled areas over the label axis, a line on each and a legend for several value fields.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:area</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">area</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/bar">&#123;chart:bar&#125;</a> <span class="about">Columns from a zero baseline.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:bar</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">bar</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/boxplot">&#123;chart:boxplot&#125;</a> <span class="about">A box per group of label.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:boxplot</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">boxplot</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/bubble">&#123;chart:bubble&#125;</a> <span class="about">A scatter whose dots have the area of size.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:bubble</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">bubble</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/bullet">&#123;chart:bullet&#125;</a> <span class="about">A track per row with a thin bar of value and target.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:bullet</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">bullet</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/calendar">&#123;chart:calendar&#125;</a> <span class="about">A square per day, a column per week.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:calendar</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/candlestick">&#123;chart:candlestick&#125;</a> <span class="about">A candle per row (label the day).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:candlestick</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">candlestick</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/chord">&#123;chart:chord&#125;</a> <span class="about">Flows between the members of one group.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:chord</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chord</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/column">&#123;chart:column&#125;</a> <span class="about">Bar by another name, drawn as bar.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:column</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">column</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/density">&#123;chart:density&#125;</a> <span class="about">A smooth curve per group of label.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:density</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/donut">&#123;chart:donut&#125;</a> <span class="about">Parts of a whole - positive values only, past eight the smallest folded into a grey &#039;Other&#039;, a legend with value and share.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:donut</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">donut</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/dual">&#123;chart:dual&#125;</a> <span class="about">Two measures on two axes.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:dual</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">dual</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/funnel">&#123;chart:funnel&#125;</a> <span class="about">The rows top to bottom as centred bars as wide as their value against the largest, joined by light bands.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:funnel</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">funnel</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">

      <a class="name" href="?tag/toy/ripples">&#123;toy:ripples&#125;</a> <span class="about">A ripple tank: waves spread over water, bounce off walls and run through each other, crest on crest and trough on trough.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toy:ripples</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">ripples</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/toy/sand">&#123;toy:sand&#125;</a> <span class="about">A falling sand box: paint with sand that piles up, water that runs and finds its level, walls, wood that burns.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toy:sand</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sand</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/toy/sorting">&#123;toy:sorting&#125;</a> <span class="about">Sorting algorithms at work on a row of bars.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toy:sorting</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sorting</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/toy/spirograph">&#123;toy:spirograph&#125;</a> <span class="about">A toothed wheel rolls round inside a ring, a pen in one of its holes, and draws the curve.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toy:spirograph</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">spirograph</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/toy/starfield">&#123;toy:starfield&#125;</a> <span class="about">Flying through space: stars come out of the dark ahead and rush past, faster the nearer they get.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toy:starfield</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">starfield</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="kind-diagram">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Diagram types</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/class">&#123;diagram:class&#125;</a> <span class="about">UML classes, a box each of the name, the fields and the methods, and the relations between them, the Mermaid way.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:class</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:class</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">class</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/class</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/er">&#123;diagram:er&#125;</a> <span class="about">Entities as tables of their attributes and the relations between them, the feet of a crow at each end for how many, the Mermaid way.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:er</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:er</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">er</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/er</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/flowchart">&#123;diagram:flowchart&#125;</a> <span class="about">Nodes and the edges between them, a line a chain.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:flowchart</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:flowchart</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">flowchart</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/flowchart</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/gitgraph">&#123;diagram:gitgraph&#125;</a> <span class="about">The commits of a repository on their branches, the branches leaving and the merges joining, the Mermaid way.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:gitgraph</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:gitgraph</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">gitgraph</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/gitgraph</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/journey">&#123;diagram:journey&#125;</a> <span class="about">The steps a person takes, how each felt and who took it, the Mermaid way.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:journey</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:journey</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">journey</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/journey</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/mindmap">&#123;diagram:mindmap&#125;</a> <span class="about">A subject in the middle and its branches round it, the hierarchy read from the indentation, the Mermaid way.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:mindmap</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:mindmap</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">mindmap</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/mindmap</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/sequence">&#123;diagram:sequence&#125;</a> <span class="about">Participants side by side, a lifeline under each, the messages between them from top to bottom.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:sequence</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:sequence</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/state">&#123;diagram:state&#125;</a> <span class="about">The states of a machine and the transitions between them, the Mermaid way.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:state</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:state</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">state</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/state</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/tree">&#123;diagram:tree&#125;</a> <span class="about">A hierarchy read from the indentation.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:tree</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:tree</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>

<section class="section" id="kind-widget">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Widgets</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/accordion">&#123;widget:accordion&#125;</a> <span class="about">Every {tab} a &lt;details&gt; with its label as the &lt;summary&gt;.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:accordion</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">tab</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Question&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/tab</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:accordion</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/breadcrumb">&#123;widget:breadcrumb&#125;</a> <span class="about">Where the page stands: a &lt;nav&gt; with an ordered list of links from the application&#039;s home to the page, the last one the page itself.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:breadcrumb</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/carousel">&#123;widget:carousel&#125;</a> <span class="about">Every {tab} a slide of a row that scrolls and snaps sideways, with previous and next links on each slide and a dot per slide below.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:carousel</span> <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Our kettles&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">tab</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Sunrise&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/tab</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">tab</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Ocean&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/tab</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:carousel</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/copy">&#123;widget:copy&#125;</a> <span class="about">The text in a &lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt; that selects as a whole with a click, and a button that puts it on the clipboard.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:copy</span> <span class="hl-var">$command</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:copy</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>npm install <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$package</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:copy</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/drawer">&#123;widget:drawer&#125;</a> <span class="about">A panel that slides in from the side of the window, with the button that opens it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:drawer</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;filters&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Filters&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">button</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Show filters&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">side</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;left&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:drawer</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/dropdown">&#123;widget:dropdown&#125;</a> <span class="about">A button and the menu it opens, on the popover attribute.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:dropdown</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Account&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;a</span> <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;?profile&quot;</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>Profile<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/a</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:dropdown</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/lightbox">&#123;widget:lightbox&#125;</a> <span class="about">A gallery: every {tab} a thumbnail in a grid, a link that opens the picture large over the page, with its caption.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:lightbox</span> <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;The harbour&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">tab</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;At dawn&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;img</span> <span class="hl-opt">src</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;dawn.jpg&quot;</span> <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;...&quot;</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/tab</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:lightbox</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/modal">&#123;widget:modal&#125;</a> <span class="about">A &lt;dialog&gt; with the content, and a button that opens it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:modal</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;terms&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Terms of sale&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">button</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Read the terms&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:modal</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/poll">&#123;widget:poll&#125;</a> <span class="about">A vote among the answers of options=, once per visitor, and then the results as bars with their percentages.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:poll</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;favorite-language&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">options</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;PHP, Python, Ruby, Go&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Your favourite language?&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/tabs">&#123;widget:tabs&#125;</a> <span class="about">Tabs without JavaScript: a radio button and a label per tab, the CSS shows the panel of the checked one.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:tabs</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">tab</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Overview&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/tab</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">tab</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Specs&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/tab</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:tabs</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/toast">&#123;widget:toast&#125;</a> <span class="about">A notice the visitor dismisses.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:toast</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Saved&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>Your changes are kept.<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:toast</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:toast</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Saved&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/tooltip">&#123;widget:tooltip&#125;</a> <span class="about">A short tip shown while the pointer is on its trigger or the trigger has the focus.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:tooltip</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Billed every month&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>Starter plan<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:tooltip</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:tooltip</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;What this means&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/wizard">&#123;widget:wizard&#125;</a> <span class="about">A form or a story in steps, one shown at a time.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:wizard</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">tab</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Address&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/tab</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">tab</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Payment&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/tab</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:wizard</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="kind-barcode">

    <div class="cheat-item">

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/code39">&#123;barcode:code39&#125;</a> <span class="about">Code 39, the barcode of industry and of warehouse labels.</span>

      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/datamatrix">&#123;barcode:datamatrix&#125;</a> <span class="about">Data Matrix ECC 200 (ISO/IEC 16022), the square code of parts, letters and medicine packs.</span>

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:ean13</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>

    <div class="cheat-item">

      <a class="name" href="?tag/input/toggle">&#123;input:toggle&#125;</a> <span class="about">A switch, on or off: a native checkbox with role=&quot;switch&quot;, drawn as a switch by its style alone, so it needs no script.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">input:toggle</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;...&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toggle</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;...&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="kind-fake">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Fake kinds</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/fake/address">&#123;fake:address&#125;</a> <span class="about">A street, a postcode and a city on one line, 42 Baker Road, 02134 Lisbon.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">fake:address</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/fake/boolean">&#123;fake:boolean&#125;</a> <span class="about">TRUE with a chance of chance= percent, 50 unless said otherwise.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">fake:boolean</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/fake/city">&#123;fake:city&#125;</a> <span class="about">A city of the world, Lisbon.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">fake:city</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/fake/colour">&#123;fake:colour&#125;</a> <span class="about">A colour as CSS writes it in hexadecimal, #3a7bd5.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">fake:colour</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/fake/company">&#123;fake:company&#125;</a> <span class="about">A company name, Walker &amp; Hill or Oslo Supplies.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">fake:company</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/fake/country">&#123;fake:country&#125;</a> <span class="about">A country by its English name, New Zealand.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">fake:country</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/fake/date">&#123;fake:date&#125;</a> <span class="about">A moment between from= and to=.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">fake:date</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/fake/decimal">&#123;fake:decimal&#125;</a> <span class="about">A number from from= to to=.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">fake:decimal</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>

  <div class="section-head"><h2>Sequence types</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
