<section class="section" id="kind-pattern">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Patterns</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern/blobs">&#123;pattern:blobs&#125;</a> <span class="about">Soft organic shapes about the size across, scattered by the seed.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:blobs</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern/checks">&#123;pattern:checks&#125;</a> <span class="about">A checkerboard of squares of the size, the dark squares in the inks, the light ones the background.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:checks</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern/chevron">&#123;pattern:chevron&#125;</a> <span class="about">Bold zigzag bands the size apart, the inks taking turns row by row, their corners sharp.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:chevron</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern/circles">&#123;pattern:circles&#125;</a> <span class="about">Circle packing: circles of the inks laid where the seed tries them, each as large as the room there allows up to the size.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:circles</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern/dots">&#123;pattern:dots&#125;</a> <span class="about">Polka dots in staggered rows, two to a tile of the size, the second in the second ink when there is one.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:dots</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern/grain">&#123;pattern:grain&#125;</a> <span class="about">Film grain: fractal noise of the browser&#039;s feTurbulence, the size its grain in pixels, in the ink over the background.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:grain</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern/grid">&#123;pattern:grid&#125;</a> <span class="about">Graph paper: a thin line every size pixels in the first ink and a bolder one every fifth in the second.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:grid</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern/hexagons">&#123;pattern:hexagons&#125;</a> <span class="about">A honeycomb of flat-topped hexagons, the size their radius, each filled with an ink the seed picks and set a little apart.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:hexagons</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern/isometric">&#123;pattern:isometric&#125;</a> <span class="about">Tumbling blocks: cubes seen from above in isometric view, the size their edge, the top in the first ink.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:isometric</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern/mesh">&#123;pattern:mesh&#125;</a> <span class="about">A soft mesh gradient: a glow of every ink, the size its radius, at a place the seed picks.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:mesh</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern/stripes">&#123;pattern:stripes&#125;</a> <span class="about">Diagonal stripes, a band of each ink half the size wide and the background between them, turned by angle=.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:stripes</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern/topography">&#123;pattern:topography&#125;</a> <span class="about">The contour lines of a landscape the seed makes up.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:topography</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern/triangles">&#123;pattern:triangles&#125;</a> <span class="about">A mosaic of equilateral triangles of the size, pointing up and down by turns.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:triangles</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern/truchet">&#123;pattern:truchet&#125;</a> <span class="about">Smith&#039;s Truchet tiles: squares of the size, each with two quarter circles joining the middles of its sides.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:truchet</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern/voronoi">&#123;pattern:voronoi&#125;</a> <span class="about">A Voronoi diagram: cells around points the seed scatters, one in every square of the size.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:voronoi</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pattern/waves">&#123;pattern:waves&#125;</a> <span class="about">Rows of wavy lines the size apart, the inks taking turns, each row shifted along the one above by a step the seed chooses.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pattern:waves</span> <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="kind-sound">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Sounds</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/bell">&#123;sound:bell&#125;</a> <span class="about">A struck bell that rings for two seconds.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:bell</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/chime">&#123;sound:chime&#125;</a> <span class="about">Two soft bells a fifth apart, the second a moment after the first.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:chime</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/click">&#123;sound:click&#125;</a> <span class="about">The short dry click of a switch or a key, a burst of noise and a tap of tone.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:click</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/coin">&#123;sound:coin&#125;</a> <span class="about">The coin of an old video game.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:coin</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/error">&#123;sound:error&#125;</a> <span class="about">Two low notes falling a tritone, buzzing and dull.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:error</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/hihat">&#123;sound:hihat&#125;</a> <span class="about">A closed hi-hat: a very short hiss of noise, all its low end filtered away.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:hihat</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/jump">&#123;sound:jump&#125;</a> <span class="about">The hop of a platform game.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:jump</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/kick">&#123;sound:kick&#125;</a> <span class="about">A bass drum: a sine that drops from 150 Hz to 45 in a tenth of a second, with a little click of noise on the beat.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:kick</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/laser">&#123;sound:laser&#125;</a> <span class="about">The zap of a space game: a square wave diving from high to low.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:laser</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/notify">&#123;sound:notify&#125;</a> <span class="about">A gentle ding-dong, G then C above it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:notify</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/pop">&#123;sound:pop&#125;</a> <span class="about">A bubble that bursts: a sine swept quickly upward, gone as soon as it is there.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:pop</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/powerup">&#123;sound:powerup&#125;</a> <span class="about">Collected something that makes you stronger.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:powerup</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/snare">&#123;sound:snare&#125;</a> <span class="about">A snare drum: a burst of noise, highpassed for the rattle of the snares, over a short falling tone for the drum&#039;s body.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:snare</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/success">&#123;sound:success&#125;</a> <span class="about">A quick rising major arpeggio, C E G C.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:success</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/tick">&#123;sound:tick&#125;</a> <span class="about">The tick of a clock: a high wooden tap, shorter and brighter than a click.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:tick</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/warning">&#123;sound:warning&#125;</a> <span class="about">Two tones taking turns, high and low, four times.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:warning</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/whoosh">&#123;sound:whoosh&#125;</a> <span class="about">Air rushing past: noise through a bandpass filter that sweeps up from low to high, swelling and fading.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:whoosh</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/tilt">&#123;effect:tilt&#125;</a> <span class="about">A card that leans in 3D when the pointer is on it or the focus is in it, and settles again when it leaves.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:tilt</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;div</span> <span class="hl-opt">class</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;card&quot;</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>...<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/div</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:tilt</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/typewriter">&#123;effect:typewriter&#125;</a> <span class="about">A line of text typed out letter by letter behind a caret, once, then the caret blinks a few times and stays.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:typewriter</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>Hello, world<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:typewriter</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:typewriter</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Hello, world&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/wave">&#123;effect:wave&#125;</a> <span class="about">The letters rise and fall one after the other, a wave running through the word.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:wave</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>Hello there<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:wave</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:wave</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Hello there&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
